def MatchingLogic.AgreeOn : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → {M : MatchingLogic.Model S} → Set M.carrier → (Var → M.carrier) → (Var → M.carrier) → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} {M} C ρ ρ' => ∀ (x : Var), ρ x = ρ' x ∧ ρ x ∈ C ∨ ρ x ∉ C ∧ ρ' x ∉ C
def MatchingLogic.AlphaFreshWitnessed : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) → Prop :=
fun {S} Gamma =>
 ∀ {x : ℕ} {p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ},
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p ∈ Gamma →
 ∃ x' p' y,
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p).AlphaEq (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x' p') ∧
 y ∉ p'.allVars ∧
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x' p').imp (MatchingLogic.Pattern.captureAvoidingSubst x' y p') ∈ Gamma
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSig : MatchingLogic.Signature :=
{ Sym := MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSym, arity := fun x => 2 }
def MatchingLogic.AppCtx.comp : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → MatchingLogic.AppCtx S Var → MatchingLogic.AppCtx S Var → MatchingLogic.AppCtx S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} outer inner => MatchingLogic.AppCtx.brecOn outer (MatchingLogic.AppCtx.comp._f inner)
def MatchingLogic.AppCtx.liftSignature : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [inst : DecidableEq S.Sym] →
 (F : Finset S.Sym) → MatchingLogic.AppCtx (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F) Var → MatchingLogic.AppCtx S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Sym] F x => MatchingLogic.AppCtx.brecOn x (MatchingLogic.AppCtx.liftSignature._f F)
def MatchingLogic.AppCtx.plug : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq Var] → MatchingLogic.AppCtx S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] x x_1 =>
 MatchingLogic.AppCtx.brecOn (motive := fun x => MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) x
 MatchingLogic.AppCtx.plug._f x_1
def MatchingLogic.AppCtx.rename : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var Var' : Type} → (Var → Var') → MatchingLogic.AppCtx S Var → MatchingLogic.AppCtx S Var' :=
fun {S} {Var Var'} f x => MatchingLogic.AppCtx.brecOn x (MatchingLogic.AppCtx.rename._f f)
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Applicative.ap : {Const Var : Type} →
 MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.Applicative.appSig Const) Var →
 MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.Applicative.appSig Const) Var →
 MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.Applicative.appSig Const) Var :=
fun {Const Var} φ ψ => MatchingLogic.Pattern.app MatchingLogic.Applicative.appSym ![φ, ψ]
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Applicative.appSig : Type → MatchingLogic.Signature :=
fun Const =>
 { Sym := Option Const,
 arity := fun s =>
 match s with
 | none => 2
 | some val => 0 }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Applicative.appSym : {Const : Type} → (MatchingLogic.Applicative.appSig Const).Sym :=
fun {Const} => none
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Applicative.coord₁ : {Const : Type} →
 MatchingLogic.Coord (MatchingLogic.Applicative.appSig Const) :=
fun {Const} => ⟨none, 0⟩
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Applicative.coord₂ : {Const : Type} →
 MatchingLogic.Coord (MatchingLogic.Applicative.appSig Const) :=
fun {Const} => ⟨none, 1⟩
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.BoxesControl.Mo : MatchingLogic.Model MatchingLogic.BoxesControl.Sg :=
{ carrier := Fin 4, nonempty := MatchingLogic.BoxesControl.Mo._proof_1,
 interp := fun x a => if a 0 = 1 ∧ a 1 = 1 then {0} else if a 0 = 2 ∧ a 1 = 3 then {1} else ∅ }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.BoxesControl.Sg : MatchingLogic.Signature :=
{ Sym := Unit, arity := fun x => 2 }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.BoxesControl.SgConst : MatchingLogic.Signature :=
{ Sym := Unit, arity := fun x => 0 }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.BoxesControl.e0 : MatchingLogic.Coord MatchingLogic.BoxesControl.Sg :=
⟨(), 0⟩
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.BoxesControl.e1 : MatchingLogic.Coord MatchingLogic.BoxesControl.Sg :=
⟨(), 1⟩
def MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier : MatchingLogic.Signature → Type :=
fun S => { Gamma // MatchingLogic.IsMCS Gamma ∧ MatchingLogic.FreshWitnessed Gamma }
@[instance_reducible] def MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier.coeSet : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 Coe (MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) (Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ)) :=
fun {S} => { coe := fun Gamma => ↑Gamma }
def MatchingLogic.CanonicalExistenceProperty : MatchingLogic.Signature → Prop :=
fun S =>
 ∀ (Gamma : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) (sigma : S.Sym) (args : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ),
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.app sigma args ∈ ↑Gamma →
 ∃ components,
 (∀ (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), args i ∈ ↑(components i)) ∧ Gamma ∈ MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp sigma components
def MatchingLogic.CaptureFree : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → Var → Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} x y x_1 => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x_1 (MatchingLogic.CaptureFree._f x y)
def MatchingLogic.Closed : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → {Var : Type} → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} φ => MatchingLogic.FV φ = ∅
def MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S → Type :=
fun {S} root => { o // o = none → MatchingLogic.Missing root }
def MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier.isStar : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} → MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root → Prop :=
fun {S} {root} point => ↑point = none
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Coord : MatchingLogic.Signature → Type :=
fun S => (σ : S.Sym) × Fin (S.arity σ)
def MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Model.ofFOCurrent : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → (A : Type) → [((MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).withConstants (Option Var)).Structure A] → A :=
fun {S} {Var} A [((MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).withConstants (Option Var)).Structure A] =>
 ↑((MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).con none)
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Model.ofFOStructure : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 (A : Type) →
 [Nonempty A] →
 [((MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).withConstants (Option Var)).Structure A] → MatchingLogic.Model S :=
fun {S} {Var} A [Nonempty A] [((MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).withConstants (Option Var)).Structure A] =>
 { carrier := A, nonempty := ⋯,
 interp := fun sigma args =>
 {out | FirstOrder.Language.Structure.RelMap (Sum.inl (MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relSym sigma)) (Fin.cons out args)} }
def MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Model.ofFOValuation : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 (A : Type) → [((MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).withConstants (Option Var)).Structure A] → Var → A :=
fun {S} {Var} A [((MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).withConstants (Option Var)).Structure A] x =>
 ↑((MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).con (some x))
def MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Model.toFOParameters : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → (Var → M.carrier) → M.carrier → Option Var → M.carrier :=
fun {S} {Var} M rho u x =>
 match x with
 | none => u
 | some x => rho x
@[instance_reducible] def MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Model.toFOStructure : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → (MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).Structure M.carrier :=
fun {S} M =>
 { funMap := fun {n} a => isEmptyElim a,
 RelMap := fun {n} r xs =>
 have ys := fun i => xs (Fin.cast ⋯ i);
 ys 0 ∈ M.interp ↑r fun i => ys i.succ }
def MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Pattern.toFOAux.{u} : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var →
 {alpha : Type u} → (Var → alpha) → alpha → (MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).Formula alpha :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] x x_1 x_2 x_3 =>
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
 (x : Type u) → (Var → x) → x → (MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).Formula x) x
 MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Pattern.toFOAux._f x_1 x_2 x_3
def MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Pattern.toFOFormula : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq Var] → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → (MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).Formula (Option Var) :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] p => MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Pattern.toFOAux p some none
def MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Pattern.toFOSentence : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → ((MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).withConstants (Option Var)).Sentence :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] p =>
 FirstOrder.Language.Formula.equivSentence (MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Pattern.toFOFormula p)
def MatchingLogic.EntryIII.counterTheory : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) →
 MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → ((MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).withConstants (Option Var)).Theory :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] Delta phi =>
 MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Pattern.toFOSentence '' Delta ∪
 {FirstOrder.Language.Formula.not (MatchingLogic.EntryIII.Pattern.toFOSentence phi)}
def MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage : MatchingLogic.Signature → FirstOrder.Language :=
fun S => { Functions := fun x => Empty, Relations := fun n => { sigma // S.arity sigma + 1 = n } }
def MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relSym : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (sigma : S.Sym) → (MatchingLogic.EntryIII.relLanguage S).Relations (S.arity sigma + 1) :=
fun {S} sigma => ⟨sigma, ⋯⟩
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSig : MatchingLogic.Signature :=
{ Sym := MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym,
 arity := fun x =>
 match x with
 | MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.nullary => 0
 | MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.unary => 1 }
def MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.HugeSig : MatchingLogic.Signature :=
{ Sym := Set ℕ, arity := fun x => 1 }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.auditModel : MatchingLogic.Model
 MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSig :=
{ carrier := Bool, nonempty := ⋯,
 interp := fun x x_1 =>
 match x, x_1 with
 | MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.nullary, x => {false}
 | MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.unary, a =>
 {a ⟨0, MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.auditModel._proof_1⟩} }
def MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.auditRho : ℕ → MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.auditModel.carrier :=
fun x => false
@[instance_reducible] def MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.instDecidableEqAuditSym : DecidableEq
 MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym :=
fun x y => if h : x.ctorIdx = y.ctorIdx then isTrue ⋯ else isFalse ⋯
@[instance_reducible] def MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.instEncodableAuditSym : Encodable
 MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym :=
Fintype.toEncodable MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym
@[instance_reducible] def MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.instFintypeAuditSym : Fintype
 MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym :=
{ elems := {MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.nullary, MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.unary},
 complete := MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.instFintypeAuditSym._proof_1 }
def MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.nullaryPattern : MatchingLogic.Pattern MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSig
 ℕ :=
MatchingLogic.Pattern.app MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.nullary fun i => i.elim0
def MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.unaryPattern : MatchingLogic.Pattern MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSig ℕ :=
MatchingLogic.Pattern.app MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.unary fun x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.var 0
def MatchingLogic.FV : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → {Var : Type} → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Set Var :=
fun {S} {Var} x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x MatchingLogic.FV._f
def MatchingLogic.FiniteLocalCompleteness : MatchingLogic.Signature → (Var : Type) → [DecidableEq Var] → Prop :=
fun S Var [DecidableEq Var] =>
 ∀ (l : List (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)) (phi : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),
 MatchingLogic.LocalCons {delta | delta ∈ l} phi → MatchingLogic.Provable ∅ ((MatchingLogic.conj l).imp phi)
def MatchingLogic.FiniteLocalModelExistence : MatchingLogic.Signature → (Var : Type) → [DecidableEq Var] → Prop :=
fun S Var [DecidableEq Var] =>
 ∀ (l : List (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)),
 MatchingLogic.LocConsistent {delta | delta ∈ l} → ∃ M rho u, u ∈ M.denoteSet rho {delta | delta ∈ l}
def MatchingLogic.FreshWitnessed : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) → Prop :=
fun {S} Gamma =>
 ∀ {x : ℕ} {p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ},
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p ∈ Gamma →
 ∃ y ∉ p.allVars, (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p).imp (MatchingLogic.Pattern.captureAvoidingSubst x y p) ∈ Gamma
def MatchingLogic.Generated : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S → MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S → Prop :=
fun {S} root world => Relation.ReflTransGen (MatchingLogic.canonicalStep root) root world
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S → Type :=
fun {S} root => { world // MatchingLogic.Generated root world }
def MatchingLogic.GlobalCons : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → [DecidableEq Var] → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] Γ φ => ∀ (M : MatchingLogic.Model S), M.SatSet Γ → M.Sat φ
def MatchingLogic.InfiniteFreshVariableSupply : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) → Prop :=
fun {S} Gamma => {y | ∀ q ∈ Gamma, y ∉ MatchingLogic.FV q}.Infinite
def MatchingLogic.InfiniteWitnessSupply : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) → Prop :=
fun {S} Gamma =>
 ∀ {x : ℕ} {p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ},
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p ∈ Gamma →
 {y | (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p).imp (MatchingLogic.Pattern.captureAvoidingSubst x y p) ∈ Gamma}.Infinite
def MatchingLogic.IsMCS : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → [DecidableEq Var] → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] Gamma =>
 MatchingLogic.LocConsistent Gamma ∧
 ∀ {Delta : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)}, Gamma ⊂ Delta → ¬MatchingLogic.LocConsistent Delta
def MatchingLogic.LocConsistent : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → [DecidableEq Var] → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] Gamma => ¬MatchingLogic.LocProvable Gamma MatchingLogic.Pattern.bot
def MatchingLogic.LocProvable : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → [DecidableEq Var] → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] Gamma phi =>
 ∃ l, (∀ delta ∈ l, delta ∈ Gamma) ∧ MatchingLogic.Provable ∅ ((MatchingLogic.conj l).imp phi)
def MatchingLogic.LocalCons : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → [DecidableEq Var] → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] Δ φ =>
 ∀ (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (ρ : Var → M.carrier), M.denoteSet ρ Δ ⊆ M.denote ρ φ
def MatchingLogic.Missing : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S → Prop :=
fun {S} root => ∃ x, ∀ (world : MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root), MatchingLogic.Pattern.var x ∉ ↑↑world
def MatchingLogic.Model.BackwardClosed : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → Set M.carrier → Prop :=
fun {S} M C => ∀ ⦃u : M.carrier⦄, u ∈ C → ∀ ⦃v : M.carrier⦄, M.Step u v → v ∈ C
def MatchingLogic.Model.Sat : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → [DecidableEq Var] → MatchingLogic.Model S → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] M φ => ∀ (ρ : Var → M.carrier), M.Total ρ φ
def MatchingLogic.Model.SatSet : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → [DecidableEq Var] → MatchingLogic.Model S → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] M Γ => ∀ γ ∈ Γ, M.Sat γ
def MatchingLogic.Model.Step : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → M.carrier → M.carrier → Prop :=
fun {S} M u v => ∃ σ a i, u ∈ M.interp σ a ∧ v = a i
def MatchingLogic.Model.Total : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq Var] → (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → (Var → M.carrier) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] M ρ φ => M.denote ρ φ = Set.univ
def MatchingLogic.Model.app : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → (σ : S.Sym) → (Fin (S.arity σ) → Set M.carrier) → Set M.carrier :=
fun {S} M σ A => {u | ∃ a, (∀ (i : Fin (S.arity σ)), a i ∈ A i) ∧ u ∈ M.interp σ a}
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Model.carrier : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → MatchingLogic.Model S → Type :=
fun S self => self.1
def MatchingLogic.Model.denote : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq Var] → (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → (Var → M.carrier) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Set M.carrier :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] M x x_1 =>
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn (motive := fun x => (Var → M.carrier) → Set M.carrier) x_1
 (MatchingLogic.Model.denote._f M) x
def MatchingLogic.Model.denoteSet : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → (Var → M.carrier) → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → Set M.carrier :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] M ρ Δ => ⋂ δ ∈ Δ, M.denote ρ δ
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Model.extendSignature : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 [inst : DecidableEq S.Sym] →
 (F : Finset S.Sym) → MatchingLogic.Model (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F) → MatchingLogic.Model S :=
fun {S} [DecidableEq S.Sym] F M =>
 { carrier := M.carrier, nonempty := ⋯,
 interp := fun sigma args => if h : sigma ∈ F then M.interp ⟨sigma, h⟩ args else ∅ }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Model.interp : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (self : MatchingLogic.Model S) → (σ : S.Sym) → (Fin (S.arity σ) → self.carrier) → Set self.carrier :=
fun S self => self.3
def MatchingLogic.Model.reachWord : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → List (MatchingLogic.Coord S) → M.carrier → M.carrier → Prop :=
fun {S} M x x_1 x_2 =>
 List.brecOn (motive := fun x => M.carrier → M.carrier → Prop) x (MatchingLogic.Model.reachWord._f M) x_1 x_2
def MatchingLogic.Model.stepAt : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → MatchingLogic.Coord S → M.carrier → M.carrier → Prop :=
fun {S} M e u v => ∃ a, u ∈ M.interp e.fst a ∧ v = a e.snd
def MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem.limit : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {sigma : S.Sym} →
 {Gamma : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} →
 {phi : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ} →
 {enum : ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ} →
 MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem sigma Gamma phi enum → Fin (S.arity sigma) → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) :=
fun {S} {sigma} {Gamma} {phi} {enum} T i => {q | ∃ k, q ∈ T.stages k i}
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem.stages : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {sigma : S.Sym} →
 {Gamma : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} →
 {phi : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ} →
 {enum : ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ} →
 MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem sigma Gamma phi enum →
 ℕ → Fin (S.arity sigma) → List (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) :=
fun S sigma Gamma phi enum self => self.1
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Necessity.C : Set (Fin 3) :=
{0}
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Necessity.M : MatchingLogic.Model MatchingLogic.Necessity.S :=
{ carrier := Fin 3, nonempty := MatchingLogic.Necessity.M._proof_1, interp := fun x a => if a 0 = 1 then {0} else ∅ }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Necessity.S : MatchingLogic.Signature :=
{ Sym := Unit, arity := fun x => 1 }
def MatchingLogic.Necessity.psi : {Var : Type} → Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern MatchingLogic.Necessity.S Var :=
fun {Var} x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.app () fun x_1 => MatchingLogic.Pattern.var x
def MatchingLogic.Necessity.rho : {Var : Type} → Var → Fin 3 :=
fun {Var} x => 1
def MatchingLogic.Necessity.rho' : {Var : Type} → Var → Fin 3 :=
fun {Var} x => 2
def MatchingLogic.PForm.Taut : MatchingLogic.PForm → Prop :=
fun p => ∀ (v : ℕ → Bool), MatchingLogic.PForm.eval v p = true
def MatchingLogic.PForm.eval : (ℕ → Bool) → MatchingLogic.PForm → Bool :=
fun v x => MatchingLogic.PForm.brecOn x (MatchingLogic.PForm.eval._f v)
def MatchingLogic.PForm.subst : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → (ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → MatchingLogic.PForm → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} θ x => MatchingLogic.PForm.brecOn x (MatchingLogic.PForm.subst._f θ)
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.AvoidsBinder : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → Prop :=
fun {S} y x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x (MatchingLogic.Pattern.AvoidsBinder._f y)
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.FreshExListTrace : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 List ℕ → List ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → Prop :=
fun {S} x x_1 x_2 =>
 List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → Prop) x MatchingLogic.Pattern.FreshExListTrace._f
 x_1 x_2
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.IsCaptureAvoidingSubst : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 ℕ → ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → Prop :=
fun {S} x y p q => ∃ p', p.AlphaEq p' ∧ MatchingLogic.CaptureFree x y p' ∧ q = MatchingLogic.substVar x y p'
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Pattern.al : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} x φ => (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x φ.nt).nt
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.allVars : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → [DecidableEq Var] → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Finset Var :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x MatchingLogic.Pattern.allVars._f
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Pattern.and : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} φ ψ => (φ.imp ψ.nt).nt
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.avoidBinder : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ :=
fun {S} y x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x (MatchingLogic.Pattern.avoidBinder._f y)
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.captureAvoidingSubst : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 ℕ → ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ :=
fun {S} x y p => MatchingLogic.substVar x y (MatchingLogic.Pattern.avoidBinder y p)
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.complexity : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {S} x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x MatchingLogic.Pattern.complexity._f
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.exList : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 List ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ :=
fun {S} x x_1 =>
 List.brecOn (motive := fun x => MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) x
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.exList._f x_1
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.fresh : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → ℕ :=
fun {S} p => (insert 0 p.allVars).max' ⋯ + 1
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.freshTuple : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {n : ℕ} → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → (Fin n → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) → Fin n → ℕ :=
fun {S} {n} p Phi i => MatchingLogic.Pattern.tupleFreshBase✝ p Phi + 1 + ↑i
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.liftSignature : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [inst : DecidableEq S.Sym] →
 (F : Finset S.Sym) →
 MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F) Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Sym] F x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x (MatchingLogic.Pattern.liftSignature._f F)
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Pattern.nt : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} φ => φ.imp MatchingLogic.Pattern.bot
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Pattern.or : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} φ ψ => φ.nt.imp ψ
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var Var' : Type} → (Var → Var') → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var' :=
fun {S} {Var Var'} f x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x (MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename._f f)
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.restrictList : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [inst : DecidableEq S.Sym] →
 (F : Finset S.Sym) →
 (l : List (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)) →
 (∀ p ∈ l, p.symbolSupport ⊆ F) → List (MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F) Var) :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Sym] F l h =>
 List.brecOn (motive := fun l =>
 (∀ p ∈ l, p.symbolSupport ⊆ F) → List (MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F) Var)) l
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.restrictList._f F) h
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.restrictSignature : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [inst : DecidableEq S.Sym] →
 (F : Finset S.Sym) →
 (p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) →
 p.symbolSupport ⊆ F → MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F) Var :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Sym] F x x_1 =>
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
 x.symbolSupport ⊆ F → MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F) Var) x
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.restrictSignature._f F) x_1

fun {T} [Encodable T.Sym] x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x MatchingLogic.Pattern.signatureNatCode._f
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.substList : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 List ℕ → List ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ :=
fun {S} x x_1 x_2 =>
 List.brecOn (motive := fun x => List ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) x
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.substList._f x_1 x_2
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.symbolSupport : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → [DecidableEq S.Sym] → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Finset S.Sym :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Sym] x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x MatchingLogic.Pattern.symbolSupport._f
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.symbolSupportList : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → [DecidableEq S.Sym] → List (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → Finset S.Sym :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Sym] l => List.foldr (fun p support => p.symbolSupport ∪ support) ∅ l
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Pattern.tp : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} => MatchingLogic.Pattern.bot.imp MatchingLogic.Pattern.bot
def MatchingLogic.Pattern.tupleAllVars : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {n : ℕ} → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ → (Fin n → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) → Finset ℕ :=
fun {S} {n} p Phi => p.allVars ∪ Finset.univ.biUnion fun i => (Phi i).allVars
def MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SGlobalCons : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var SVar : Type} →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 Set (MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SPattern S Var SVar) → MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SPattern S Var SVar → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var SVar} [DecidableEq Var] Γ φ =>
 ∀ (M : MatchingLogic.Model S), (∀ γ ∈ Γ, MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SSat M γ) → MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SSat M φ
def MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SSat : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var SVar : Type} →
 [DecidableEq Var] → MatchingLogic.Model S → MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SPattern S Var SVar → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var SVar} [DecidableEq Var] M φ =>
 ∀ (ρ : MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SVal M Var SVar), MatchingLogic.SetVariables.sdenote M ρ φ = Set.univ
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SVal.elem : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {M : MatchingLogic.Model S} → {Var SVar : Type} → MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SVal M Var SVar → Var → M.carrier :=
fun S M Var SVar self => self.1
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SVal.sets : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {M : MatchingLogic.Model S} → {Var SVar : Type} → MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SVal M Var SVar → SVar → Set M.carrier :=
fun S M Var SVar self => self.2
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.SetVariables.constSig : MatchingLogic.Signature :=
{ Sym := Unit, arity := fun x => 0 }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.SetVariables.dPat : {Var : Type} →
 MatchingLogic.Pattern MatchingLogic.SetVariables.constSig Var :=
fun {Var} => MatchingLogic.Pattern.app () fun i => i.elim0
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.SetVariables.fullConstModel : MatchingLogic.Model MatchingLogic.SetVariables.constSig :=
{ carrier := Unit, nonempty := ⋯, interp := fun x x_1 => Set.univ }
def MatchingLogic.SetVariables.sdenote : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var SVar : Type} →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) →
 MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SVal M Var SVar → MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SPattern S Var SVar → Set M.carrier :=
fun {S} {Var SVar} [DecidableEq Var] M x x_1 =>
 MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SPattern.brecOn (motive := fun x =>
 MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SVal M Var SVar → Set M.carrier) x_1 (MatchingLogic.SetVariables.sdenote._f M) x
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Signature.Sym : MatchingLogic.Signature → Type :=
fun self => self.1
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Signature.arity : (self : MatchingLogic.Signature) → self.Sym → ℕ :=
fun self => self.2
def MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 [DecidableEq S.Sym] → Finset S.Sym → MatchingLogic.Signature :=
fun {S} [DecidableEq S.Sym] F => { Sym := ↥F, arity := fun sigma => S.arity ↑sigma }
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.Feeds : (S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature) → S.Srt → S.Srt → Prop :=
fun S s t => s = t ∨ ∃ σ i, S.argSort σ i = s ∧ S.resSort σ = t
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.FeedsStar : (S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature) → S.Srt → S.Srt → Prop :=
fun S => Relation.ReflTransGen (MatchingLogic.Sorted.Feeds S)
@[irreducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MAppCtx.plug : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 {sh st : S.Srt} →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MAppCtx S Var sh st →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var sh → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var st :=
fun {S} {Var} {sh st} a a_1 => MatchingLogic.Sorted.MAppCtx.plug._unary ⟨sh, ⟨st, ⟨a, a_1⟩⟩⟩
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MCaptureFree : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} → S.Srt → Var → Var → {s : S.Srt} → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} s' x y x_1 x_2 => MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.brecOn x_2 (MatchingLogic.Sorted.MCaptureFree._f s' x y)
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MFV : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} → {s : S.Srt} → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s → Set (S.Srt × Var) :=
fun {S} {Var} x x_1 => MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.brecOn x_1 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MFV._f
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MGlobalCons : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq S.Srt] →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 {sΓ sφ : S.Srt} →
 Set (MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var sΓ) → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var sφ → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Srt] [DecidableEq Var] {sΓ sφ} Γ φ =>
 ∀ (M : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel S), M.SatSet Γ → M.Sat φ
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MGlobalConsHet : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq S.Srt] →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 Set ((s : S.Srt) × MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s) →
 {sφ : S.Srt} → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var sφ → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Srt] [DecidableEq Var] Γ {sφ} φ =>
 ∀ (M : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel S), M.SatSetHet Γ → M.Sat φ
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel.Sat : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq S.Srt] →
 [DecidableEq Var] → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel S → {s : S.Srt} → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Srt] [DecidableEq Var] M {s} φ =>
 ∀ (ρ : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MVal M Var), MatchingLogic.Sorted.mdenote M ρ φ = Set.univ
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel.SatSet : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq S.Srt] →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel S → {s : S.Srt} → Set (MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s) → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Srt] [DecidableEq Var] M {s} Γ => ∀ γ ∈ Γ, M.Sat γ
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel.SatSetHet : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq S.Srt] →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel S → Set ((s : S.Srt) × MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s) → Prop :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Srt] [DecidableEq Var] M Γ => ∀ p ∈ Γ, M.Sat p.snd
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel.app : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel S) →
 (σ : S.Sym) → ((i : Fin (S.arity σ)) → Set (M.carrier (S.argSort σ i))) → Set (M.carrier (S.resSort σ)) :=
fun {S} M σ A => {u | ∃ a, (∀ (i : Fin (S.arity σ)), a i ∈ A i) ∧ u ∈ M.interp σ a}
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel.carrier : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel S → S.Srt → Type :=
fun S self => self.1
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel.interp : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 (self : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel S) →
 (σ : S.Sym) → ((i : Fin (S.arity σ)) → self.carrier (S.argSort σ i)) → Set (self.carrier (S.resSort σ)) :=
fun S self => self.3
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.al : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 {s : S.Srt} → Var → S.Srt → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s :=
fun {S} {Var} {s} x s' φ => (MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.ex x s' φ.nt).nt
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.and : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 {s : S.Srt} →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s :=
fun {S} {Var} {s} φ ψ => (φ.imp ψ.nt).nt
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.iff : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 {s : S.Srt} →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s :=
fun {S} {Var} {s} φ ψ => (φ.imp ψ).and (ψ.imp φ)
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.nt : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} → {s : S.Srt} → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s :=
fun {S} {Var} {s} φ => φ.imp MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.bot
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.orP : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 {s : S.Srt} →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s :=
fun {S} {Var} {s} φ ψ => φ.nt.imp ψ
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.tp : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} → {s : S.Srt} → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s :=
fun {S} {Var} {s} => MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.bot.imp MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.bot
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature.Srt : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature → Type :=
fun self => self.1
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature.Sym : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature → Type :=
fun self => self.2
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature.argSort : (self : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature) →
 (σ : self.Sym) → Fin (self.arity σ) → self.Srt :=
fun self => self.4
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature.arity : (self : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature) → self.Sym → ℕ :=
fun self => self.3
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature.resSort : (self : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature) →
 self.Sym → self.Srt :=
fun self => self.5
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSoundness : (S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature) →
 (Var : Type) → [DecidableEq S.Srt] → [DecidableEq Var] → Prop :=
fun S Var [DecidableEq S.Srt] [DecidableEq Var] =>
 ∀ {sΓ sφ : S.Srt} (Γ : Set (MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var sΓ)) (φ : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var sφ),
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MProvable Γ φ → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MGlobalCons Γ φ
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.MVal : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel S → Type → Type :=
fun {S} M Var => (s : S.Srt) → Var → M.carrier s
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.S3 : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature :=
{ Srt := MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3, Sym := MatchingLogic.Sorted.Sym3, arity := fun x => 1,
 argSort := fun x x_1 => MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.b,
 resSort := fun σ =>
 match σ with
 | MatchingLogic.Sorted.Sym3.f => MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.a
 | MatchingLogic.Sorted.Sym3.g => MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.c }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.fAp : {Var : Type} →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern MatchingLogic.Sorted.S3 Var MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.b →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern MatchingLogic.Sorted.S3 Var MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.a :=
fun {Var} ψ => MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.app MatchingLogic.Sorted.Sym3.f fun x => ψ
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.gAp : {Var : Type} →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern MatchingLogic.Sorted.S3 Var MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.b →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern MatchingLogic.Sorted.S3 Var MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.c :=
fun {Var} ψ => MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.app MatchingLogic.Sorted.Sym3.g fun x => ψ
@[instance_reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.instDecidableEqSrt3 : DecidableEq MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3 :=
fun x y => if h : x.ctorIdx = y.ctorIdx then isTrue ⋯ else isFalse ⋯
@[instance_reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.instDecidableEqSym3 : DecidableEq MatchingLogic.Sorted.Sym3 :=
fun x y => if h : x.ctorIdx = y.ctorIdx then isTrue ⋯ else isFalse ⋯
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.mdenote : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq S.Srt] →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel S) →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MVal M Var → {s : S.Srt} → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s → Set (M.carrier s) :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Srt] [DecidableEq Var] M ρ x x_1 =>
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.brecOn (motive := fun x x_2 => MatchingLogic.Sorted.MVal M Var → Set (M.carrier x)) x_1
 (MatchingLogic.Sorted.mdenote._f M) ρ
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.msubstVar : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq S.Srt] →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 S.Srt →
 Var → Var → {s : S.Srt} → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Srt] [DecidableEq Var] s' x y x_1 x_2 =>
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.brecOn x_2 (MatchingLogic.Sorted.msubstVar._f s' x y)
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.mupdate : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 [DecidableEq S.Srt] →
 [DecidableEq Var] →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel S) →
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MVal M Var → (s' : S.Srt) → Var → M.carrier s' → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MVal M Var :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq S.Srt] [DecidableEq Var] M ρ s' x a t y =>
 if y = x then if ht : t = s' then ⋯ ▸ a else ρ t y else ρ t y
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.singletonM3 : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MModel MatchingLogic.Sorted.S3 :=
{ carrier := fun x => PUnit.{1}, nonempty := ⋯, interp := fun x x_1 => Set.univ }
def MatchingLogic.Sorted.substPF : {S : MatchingLogic.Sorted.MSignature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 {s : S.Srt} →
 (ℕ → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s) → MatchingLogic.PForm → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern S Var s :=
fun {S} {Var} {s} θ x => MatchingLogic.PForm.brecOn x (MatchingLogic.Sorted.substPF._f θ)
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.Γ3 : {Var : Type} →
 Var → Var → Set (MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern MatchingLogic.Sorted.S3 Var MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.a) :=
fun {Var} x y =>
 {MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.al x MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.b
 (MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.al y MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.b
 (MatchingLogic.Sorted.fAp
 ((MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.var x MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.b).and
 (MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.var y MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.b))))}
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.Sorted.φ3 : {Var : Type} →
 Var → Var → MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern MatchingLogic.Sorted.S3 Var MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.c :=
fun {Var} x y =>
 MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.al x MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.b
 (MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.al y MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.b
 ((MatchingLogic.Sorted.gAp (MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.var x MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.b)).iff
 (MatchingLogic.Sorted.gAp (MatchingLogic.Sorted.MPattern.var y MatchingLogic.Sorted.Srt3.b))))
def MatchingLogic.Soundness : MatchingLogic.Signature → (Var : Type) → [DecidableEq Var] → Prop :=
fun S Var [DecidableEq Var] =>
 ∀ (Γ : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)) (φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),
 MatchingLogic.Provable Γ φ → MatchingLogic.GlobalCons Γ φ
def MatchingLogic.StrongLocalCompleteness : MatchingLogic.Signature → (Var : Type) → [DecidableEq Var] → Prop :=
fun S Var [DecidableEq Var] =>
 ∀ (Δ : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)) (φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),
 MatchingLogic.LocalCons Δ φ → ∃ l, (∀ δ ∈ l, δ ∈ Δ) ∧ MatchingLogic.Provable ∅ ((MatchingLogic.conj l).imp φ)
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.WitnessCollapseSig : MatchingLogic.Signature :=
{ Sym := Empty, arity := fun e => e.elim }
def MatchingLogic.Witnessed : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) → Prop :=
fun {S} Gamma =>
 ∀ {x : ℕ} {p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ},
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p ∈ Gamma →
 ∃ y, (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p).imp (MatchingLogic.Pattern.captureAvoidingSubst x y p) ∈ Gamma
def MatchingLogic.alphaBlocked : MatchingLogic.Pattern MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSig ℕ :=
MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex 1
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.app MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSym.pair
 (MatchingLogic.pairArgs (MatchingLogic.Pattern.var 1) (MatchingLogic.Pattern.var 0)))
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.alphaCornerSelectorModel : MatchingLogic.Model MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSig :=
{ carrier := Bool, nonempty := ⋯, interp := fun x a => if a 0 = false ∧ a 1 = true then {true} else ∅ }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.alphaFirstSelectorModel : MatchingLogic.Model MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSig :=
{ carrier := Bool, nonempty := ⋯, interp := fun x a => if a 0 = true ∧ a 1 = false then {true} else ∅ }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.alphaUnitEmptyModel : MatchingLogic.Model MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSig :=
{ carrier := Unit, nonempty := ⋯, interp := fun x x_1 => ∅ }
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.alphaUnitFullModel : MatchingLogic.Model MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSig :=
{ carrier := Unit, nonempty := ⋯, interp := fun x x_1 => {()} }
def MatchingLogic.alphaUnitRho : ℕ → MatchingLogic.alphaUnitEmptyModel.carrier :=
fun x => ()
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.alphaWitnessModel : MatchingLogic.Model MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSig :=
{ carrier := Bool, nonempty := ⋯, interp := fun x a => if a 0 = true ∧ a 1 = true then {true} else ∅ }
def MatchingLogic.alphaWitnessRho : ℕ → MatchingLogic.alphaWitnessModel.carrier :=
fun n => decide (n = 0)
def MatchingLogic.alphaWitnessTheory : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSig ℕ) :=
{p | true ∈ MatchingLogic.alphaWitnessModel.denote MatchingLogic.alphaWitnessRho p}
def MatchingLogic.box : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → MatchingLogic.Coord S → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} e ψ => (MatchingLogic.dia e (ψ.imp MatchingLogic.Pattern.bot)).imp MatchingLogic.Pattern.bot
def MatchingLogic.boxes : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → List (MatchingLogic.Coord S) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} x x_1 =>
 List.brecOn (motive := fun x => MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) x MatchingLogic.boxes._f
 x_1
def MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (sigma : S.Sym) → (Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) → Set (MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) :=
fun {S} sigma components =>
 {Gamma |
 ∀ (args : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ),
 (∀ (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), args i ∈ ↑(components i)) → MatchingLogic.Pattern.app sigma args ∈ ↑Gamma}
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.canonicalModel : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S → MatchingLogic.Model S :=
fun {S} root => { carrier := MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S, nonempty := ⋯, interp := MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp }
def MatchingLogic.canonicalStep : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S → MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S → MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S → Prop :=
fun {S} root parent child => (MatchingLogic.canonicalModel root).Step parent child
def MatchingLogic.completedEmbed : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) →
 MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root → MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root :=
fun {S} root world => ⟨some world, ⋯⟩
def MatchingLogic.completedInterp : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) →
 (sigma : S.Sym) →
 (Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root) → Set (MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root) :=
fun {S} root sigma inputs =>
 {output |
 ∃ components,
 (∀ (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), inputs i = MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root (components i)) ∧
 ((∃ world,
 output = MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root world ∧
 world ∈ MatchingLogic.generatedInterp root sigma components) ∨
 output.isStar ∧ ∃ i, components i = MatchingLogic.generatedRoot root)}
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.completedModel : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S → MatchingLogic.Model S :=
fun {S} root =>
 { carrier := MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root, nonempty := ⋯, interp := MatchingLogic.completedInterp root }
def MatchingLogic.completedStar : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} → MatchingLogic.Missing root → MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root :=
fun {S} {root} hmissing => ⟨none, ⋯⟩
def MatchingLogic.completedValuation : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) → ℕ → MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root :=
fun {S} root x =>
 if h : ∃ world, MatchingLogic.Pattern.var x ∈ ↑↑world then MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root (Classical.choose h)
 else ⟨none, ⋯⟩
def MatchingLogic.conj : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → List (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} x => List.brecOn x MatchingLogic.conj._f
def MatchingLogic.cover : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → (C : Set M.carrier) → C.Nonempty → MatchingLogic.Model S :=
fun {S} M C hne => { carrier := ↑C × Bool, nonempty := ⋯, interp := MatchingLogic.coverInterp M C }
def MatchingLogic.coverInterp : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → (C : Set M.carrier) → (σ : S.Sym) → (Fin (S.arity σ) → ↑C × Bool) → Set (↑C × Bool) :=
fun {S} M C σ A => {p | (∀ (j : Fin (S.arity σ)), (A j).2 = p.2) ∧ ↑p.1 ∈ M.interp σ fun j => ↑(A j).1}
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.defSig : MatchingLogic.Signature → MatchingLogic.Signature :=
fun S =>
 { Sym := Option S.Sym,
 arity := fun s =>
 match s with
 | none => 1
 | some σ => S.arity σ }
def MatchingLogic.defined : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.defSig S) Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.defSig S) Var :=
fun {S} {Var} φ => MatchingLogic.Pattern.app none fun x => φ
def MatchingLogic.definednessAxiom : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.defSig S) Var :=
fun {S} {Var} x => MatchingLogic.defined (MatchingLogic.Pattern.var x)
def MatchingLogic.dia : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → MatchingLogic.Coord S → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} e ψ => MatchingLogic.Pattern.app e.fst fun j => if j = e.snd then ψ else MatchingLogic.Pattern.tp
def MatchingLogic.doubleNegIntro : MatchingLogic.PForm :=
(MatchingLogic.PForm.atom 0).imp
 (((MatchingLogic.PForm.atom 0).imp MatchingLogic.PForm.bot).imp MatchingLogic.PForm.bot)
def MatchingLogic.emb : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.defSig S) Var :=
fun {S} {Var} x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x MatchingLogic.emb._f
def MatchingLogic.expand : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 MatchingLogic.Model S → MatchingLogic.Model (MatchingLogic.defSig S) :=
fun {S} M =>
 { carrier := M.carrier, nonempty := ⋯,
 interp := fun s =>
 match (motive :=
 (s : (MatchingLogic.defSig S).Sym) → (Fin ((MatchingLogic.defSig S).arity s) → M.carrier) → Set M.carrier)
 s with
 | none => fun x => Set.univ
 | some σ => fun a => M.interp σ a }
def MatchingLogic.generatedInterp : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) →
 (sigma : S.Sym) →
 (Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root) → Set (MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root) :=
fun {S} root sigma components => {world | ↑world ∈ MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp sigma fun i => ↑(components i)}
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.generatedModel : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S → MatchingLogic.Model S :=
fun {S} root =>
 { carrier := MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root, nonempty := ⋯, interp := MatchingLogic.generatedInterp root }
def MatchingLogic.generatedRoot : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) → MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root :=
fun {S} root => ⟨root, ⋯⟩
def MatchingLogic.generatingContext : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 List (MatchingLogic.Coord S) → MatchingLogic.AppCtx S ℕ :=
fun {S} path => List.foldl (fun C e => C.comp (MatchingLogic.generatingStepContext e)) MatchingLogic.AppCtx.hole path
def MatchingLogic.generatingStepContext : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 MatchingLogic.Coord S → MatchingLogic.AppCtx S ℕ :=
fun {S} e => MatchingLogic.AppCtx.node e.fst e.snd (fun x => MatchingLogic.Pattern.tp) MatchingLogic.AppCtx.hole
def MatchingLogic.implicationTransitivity : MatchingLogic.PForm :=
((MatchingLogic.PForm.atom 0).imp (MatchingLogic.PForm.atom 1)).imp
 (((MatchingLogic.PForm.atom 1).imp (MatchingLogic.PForm.atom 2)).imp
 ((MatchingLogic.PForm.atom 0).imp (MatchingLogic.PForm.atom 2)))
@[instance_reducible] def MatchingLogic.instDecidableEqAlphaWitnessSym : DecidableEq MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSym :=
fun x y => if h : x.ctorIdx = y.ctorIdx then isTrue ⋯ else isFalse ⋯
@[instance_reducible] def MatchingLogic.instDecidableEqPForm : DecidableEq MatchingLogic.PForm :=
MatchingLogic.instDecidableEqPForm.decEq
def MatchingLogic.instDecidableEqPForm.decEq : (x x_1 : MatchingLogic.PForm) → Decidable (x = x_1) :=
fun x x_1 =>
 MatchingLogic.PForm.brecOn (motive := fun x => (x_2 : MatchingLogic.PForm) → Decidable (x = x_2)) x
 MatchingLogic.instDecidableEqPForm.decEq._f x_1

 {Var : Type} → DecidableEq (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) :=
fun {S} {Var} => Classical.decEq (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)

 DecidableEq (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) :=
fun {S} => Classical.decEq (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ)

 DecidableEq (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) :=
fun {S} => Classical.decEq (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ)

@[instance_reducible] def MatchingLogic.instFintypeAlphaWitnessSym : Fintype MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSym :=
{ elems := {MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSym.pair}, complete := MatchingLogic.instFintypeAlphaWitnessSym._proof_1 }
def MatchingLogic.localize : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) :=
fun {S} {Var} Γ => {ψ | ∃ γ ∈ Γ, ∃ p, ψ = MatchingLogic.boxes p γ}
def MatchingLogic.pairArgs : MatchingLogic.Pattern MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSig ℕ →
 MatchingLogic.Pattern MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSig ℕ →
 Fin 2 → MatchingLogic.Pattern MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSig ℕ :=
fun p q x =>
 match x with
 | ⟨0, isLt⟩ => p
 | ⟨1, isLt⟩ => q
def MatchingLogic.proj : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) → (C : Set M.carrier) → M.carrier → Bool → ↑C × Bool → M.carrier :=
fun {S} M C star i p => if p.2 = i then ↑p.1 else star
def MatchingLogic.substVar : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} → [DecidableEq Var] → Var → Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var :=
fun {S} {Var} [DecidableEq Var] x y x_1 => MatchingLogic.Pattern.brecOn x_1 (MatchingLogic.substVar._f x y)
@[reducible] def MatchingLogic.witnessCollapseModel : MatchingLogic.Model MatchingLogic.WitnessCollapseSig :=
{ carrier := Bool, nonempty := ⋯, interp := fun e => Empty.elim e }
def MatchingLogic.witnessCollapseRho : ℕ → MatchingLogic.witnessCollapseModel.carrier :=
fun n => decide (n = 0)
def MatchingLogic.witnessCollapseTheory : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern MatchingLogic.WitnessCollapseSig ℕ) :=
{p | true ∈ MatchingLogic.witnessCollapseModel.denote MatchingLogic.witnessCollapseRho p}
@[instance_reducible] def MatchingLogic.witnessSupplyDecidableEqPattern : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 DecidableEq (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ) :=
fun {S} => Classical.decEq (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ)
@MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSym.rec : {motive : MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSym → Sort u_1} →
 motive MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSym.pair → (t : MatchingLogic.AlphaWitnessSym) → motive t
@MatchingLogic.AppCtx.rec : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 {motive : MatchingLogic.AppCtx S Var → Sort u_1} →
 motive MatchingLogic.AppCtx.hole →
 ((σ : S.Sym) →
 (i : Fin (S.arity σ)) →
 (a : Fin (S.arity σ) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) →
 (a_1 : MatchingLogic.AppCtx S Var) → motive a_1 → motive (MatchingLogic.AppCtx.node σ i a a_1)) →
 (t : MatchingLogic.AppCtx S Var) → motive t
@MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.rec : {motive : MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym → Sort u_1} →
 motive MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.nullary →
 motive MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.unary → (t : MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym) → motive t
@MatchingLogic.GeneratingPath.rec : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S}
 {motive :
 (a : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) →
 (a_1 : List (MatchingLogic.Coord S)) → MatchingLogic.GeneratingPath root a a_1 → Prop},
 motive root [] ⋯ →
 (∀ {parent : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} {path : List (MatchingLogic.Coord S)} {sigma : S.Sym}
 {components : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} {i : Fin (S.arity sigma)}
 (a : MatchingLogic.GeneratingPath root parent path)
 (a_1 : parent ∈ MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp sigma components),
 motive parent path a → motive (components i) (path ++ [⟨sigma, i⟩]) ⋯) →
 ∀ {a : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} {a_1 : List (MatchingLogic.Coord S)}
 (t : MatchingLogic.GeneratingPath root a a_1), motive a a_1 t
@MatchingLogic.Model.rec : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {motive : MatchingLogic.Model S → Sort u_1} →
 ((carrier : Type) →
 (nonempty : Nonempty carrier) →
 (interp : (σ : S.Sym) → (Fin (S.arity σ) → carrier) → Set carrier) →
 motive { carrier := carrier, nonempty := nonempty, interp := interp }) →
 (t : MatchingLogic.Model S) → motive t
@MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem.rec : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {sigma : S.Sym} →
 {Gamma : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} →
 {phi : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ} →
 {enum : ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ} →
 {motive : MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem sigma Gamma phi enum → Sort u_1} →
 ((stages : ℕ → Fin (S.arity sigma) → List (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ)) →
 (base : ∀ (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), phi i ∈ stages 0 i) →
 (mono : ∀ (k : ℕ) (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), ∀ q ∈ stages k i, q ∈ stages (k + 1) i) →
 (app_mem :
 ∀ (k : ℕ),
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.app sigma fun i => MatchingLogic.conj (stages k i)) ∈ ↑Gamma) →
 (decide :
 ∀ (k : ℕ) (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)),
 enum k ∈ stages (k + 1) i ∨ (enum k).nt ∈ stages (k + 1) i) →
 (witness :
 ∀ (k : ℕ) (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)) (x : ℕ) (p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ),
 enum k = MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p →
 ∃ y ∉ p.allVars,
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p).imp
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.captureAvoidingSubst x y p) ∈
 stages (k + 1) i) →
 motive
 { stages := stages, base := base, mono := mono, app_mem := app_mem, decide := decide,
 witness := witness }) →
 (t : MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem sigma Gamma phi enum) → motive t
@MatchingLogic.PForm.rec : {motive : MatchingLogic.PForm → Sort u_1} →
 ((a : ℕ) → motive (MatchingLogic.PForm.atom a)) →
 motive MatchingLogic.PForm.bot →
 ((a a_1 : MatchingLogic.PForm) → motive a → motive a_1 → motive (a.imp a_1)) →
 (t : MatchingLogic.PForm) → motive t
@MatchingLogic.Pattern.rec : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var : Type} →
 {motive : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var → Sort u_1} →
 ((a : Var) → motive (MatchingLogic.Pattern.var a)) →
 ((σ : S.Sym) →
 (a : Fin (S.arity σ) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) →
 ((a_1 : Fin (S.arity σ)) → motive (a a_1)) → motive (MatchingLogic.Pattern.app σ a)) →
 ((a a_1 : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → motive a → motive a_1 → motive (a.imp a_1)) →
 motive MatchingLogic.Pattern.bot →
 ((a : Var) → (a_1 : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → motive a_1 → motive (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex a a_1)) →
 (t : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → motive t
@MatchingLogic.Pattern.AlphaEq.rec : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {p q : MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ} →
 {motive : p.AlphaEq q → Sort u_1} →
 ((forward : ∀ (Gamma : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ)), MatchingLogic.Provable Gamma (p.imp q)) →
 (backward : ∀ (Gamma : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ)), MatchingLogic.Provable Gamma (q.imp p)) →
 (complexity_eq : p.complexity = q.complexity) → motive ⋯) →
 (t : p.AlphaEq q) → motive t
@MatchingLogic.Provable.rec : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 {Γ : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)}
 {motive : (a : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) → MatchingLogic.Provable Γ a → Prop},
 (∀ {φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var} (a : φ ∈ Γ), motive φ ⋯) →
 (∀ {p : MatchingLogic.PForm} {θ : ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var} (a : p.Taut),
 motive (MatchingLogic.PForm.subst θ p) ⋯) →
 (∀ {φ₁ φ₂ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var} (a : MatchingLogic.Provable Γ φ₁)
 (a_1 : MatchingLogic.Provable Γ (φ₁.imp φ₂)), motive φ₁ a → motive (φ₁.imp φ₂) a_1 → motive φ₂ ⋯) →
 (∀ {x y : Var} {φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var} (a : MatchingLogic.CaptureFree x y φ),
 motive ((MatchingLogic.substVar x y φ).imp (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x φ)) ⋯) →
 (∀ {x : Var} {φ₁ φ₂ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var} (a : MatchingLogic.Provable Γ (φ₁.imp φ₂))
 (a_1 : x ∉ MatchingLogic.FV φ₂),
 motive (φ₁.imp φ₂) a → motive ((MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x φ₁).imp φ₂) ⋯) →
 (∀ {σ : S.Sym} {i : Fin (S.arity σ)} {args : Fin (S.arity σ) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var},
 motive
 ((MatchingLogic.Pattern.app σ (Function.update args i MatchingLogic.Pattern.bot)).imp
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.bot)
 ⋯) →
 (∀ {σ : S.Sym} {i : Fin (S.arity σ)} {args : Fin (S.arity σ) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var}
 {φ₁ φ₂ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var},
 motive
 ((MatchingLogic.Pattern.app σ (Function.update args i (φ₁.or φ₂))).imp
 ((MatchingLogic.Pattern.app σ (Function.update args i φ₁)).or
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.app σ (Function.update args i φ₂))))
 ⋯) →
 (∀ {σ : S.Sym} {i : Fin (S.arity σ)} {args : Fin (S.arity σ) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var} {x : Var}
 {φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var}
 (a : ∀ (j : Fin (S.arity σ)), j ≠ i → x ∉ MatchingLogic.FV (args j)),
 motive
 ((MatchingLogic.Pattern.app σ (Function.update args i (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x φ))).imp
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x (MatchingLogic.Pattern.app σ (Function.update args i φ))))
 ⋯) →
 (∀ {σ : S.Sym} {i : Fin (S.arity σ)} {args : Fin (S.arity σ) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var}
 {φ₁ φ₂ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var} (a : MatchingLogic.Provable Γ (φ₁.imp φ₂)),
 motive (φ₁.imp φ₂) a →
 motive
 ((MatchingLogic.Pattern.app σ (Function.update args i φ₁)).imp
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.app σ (Function.update args i φ₂)))
 ⋯) →
 (∀ {x : Var}, motive (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x (MatchingLogic.Pattern.var x)) ⋯) →
 (∀ {x : Var} {φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var} (C₁ C₂ : MatchingLogic.AppCtx S Var),
 motive
 ((C₁.plug ((MatchingLogic.Pattern.var x).and φ)).imp
 (C₂.plug ((MatchingLogic.Pattern.var x).and φ.nt)).nt)
 ⋯) →
 ∀ {a : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var} (t : MatchingLogic.Provable Γ a), motive a t
@MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SPattern.rec : {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} →
 {Var SVar : Type} →
 {motive : MatchingLogic.SetVariables.SPattern S Var SVar → Sort u_1} →

 Nonempty (MatchingLogic.completedModel root).carrier ∧
 MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root (MatchingLogic.generatedRoot root) ∈ Set.univ

 (f : Fin (S.arity σ) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),
 MatchingLogic.FV (MatchingLogic.Pattern.app σ f) = ⋃ i, MatchingLogic.FV (f i)

 MatchingLogic.FV MatchingLogic.Pattern.bot = ∅

 (ψ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var), MatchingLogic.FV (MatchingLogic.dia e ψ) = MatchingLogic.FV ψ

 (φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var), MatchingLogic.FV (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x φ) = MatchingLogic.FV φ \ {x}
@MatchingLogic.FV_imp : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [DecidableEq Var]
 (φ ψ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var), MatchingLogic.FV (φ.imp ψ) = MatchingLogic.FV φ ∪ MatchingLogic.FV ψ

@MatchingLogic.Model.Sat.congr_simp : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} {inst : DecidableEq Var}
 [inst_1 : DecidableEq Var] (M M_1 : MatchingLogic.Model S),
 M = M_1 → ∀ (φ φ_1 : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var), φ = φ_1 → M.Sat φ = M_1.Sat φ_1
@MatchingLogic.Model.app_const : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (σ : S.Sym)
 (h : S.arity σ = 0) (A : Fin (S.arity σ) → Set M.carrier), M.app σ A = M.interp σ fun i => absurd ⋯ ⋯
@MatchingLogic.Model.app_eq_empty : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (σ : S.Sym)
 (A : Fin (S.arity σ) → Set M.carrier) (i : Fin (S.arity σ)), A i = ∅ → M.app σ A = ∅
@MatchingLogic.Model.app_extendSignature : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} [inst : DecidableEq S.Sym] (F : Finset S.Sym)
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F)) (sigma : (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F).Sym)
 (A : Fin (S.arity ↑sigma) → Set M.carrier), (MatchingLogic.Model.extendSignature F M).app (↑sigma) A = M.app sigma A
@MatchingLogic.Model.app_inter_backwardClosed : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {M : MatchingLogic.Model S}
 {C : Set M.carrier},
 M.BackwardClosed C →
 ∀ (σ : S.Sym) (A : Fin (S.arity σ) → Set M.carrier), M.app σ A ∩ C = (M.app σ fun i => A i ∩ C) ∩ C
@MatchingLogic.Model.denote.congr_simp : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} {inst : DecidableEq Var}
 [inst_1 : DecidableEq Var] (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (a a_1 : Var → M.carrier),
 a = a_1 →
 ∀ (a_2 a_3 : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),
 a_2 = a_3 → ∀ (a_4 a_5 : M.carrier), a_4 = a_5 → M.denote a a_2 a_4 = M.denote a_1 a_3 a_5
@MatchingLogic.Model.denoteSet.congr_simp : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} {inst : DecidableEq Var}
 [inst_1 : DecidableEq Var] (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (ρ ρ_1 : Var → M.carrier),
 ρ = ρ_1 →
 ∀ (Δ Δ_1 : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)),
 Δ = Δ_1 → ∀ (a a_1 : M.carrier), a = a_1 → M.denoteSet ρ Δ a = M.denoteSet ρ_1 Δ_1 a_1
@MatchingLogic.Model.denoteSet_extendSignature_liftSignature_list : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type}
 [inst : DecidableEq Var] [inst_1 : DecidableEq S.Sym] (F : Finset S.Sym)
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F)) (rho : Var → M.carrier)
 (l : List (MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F) Var)),
 (MatchingLogic.Model.extendSignature F M).denoteSet rho
 {delta | delta ∈ List.map (MatchingLogic.Pattern.liftSignature F) l} =
 M.denoteSet rho {delta | delta ∈ l}
@MatchingLogic.Model.denoteSet_renameEquiv : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var Var' : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 [inst_1 : DecidableEq Var'] (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (e : Var ≃ Var') (rho : Var' → M.carrier)
 (Delta : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)),
 M.denoteSet rho (MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename ⇑e '' Delta) = M.denoteSet (rho ∘ ⇑e) Delta
@MatchingLogic.Model.denoteSet_renameInjective : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var Var' : Type}
 [inst : DecidableEq Var] [inst_1 : DecidableEq Var'] (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (f : Var → Var'),
 Function.Injective f →
 ∀ (rho : Var' → M.carrier) (Delta : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)),
 M.denoteSet rho (MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename f '' Delta) = M.denoteSet (rho ∘ f) Delta

 (rho : ℕ → M.carrier) (x y : ℕ) (p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ),
 M.denote rho (MatchingLogic.Pattern.captureAvoidingSubst x y p) = M.denote (Function.update rho x (rho y)) p

 M.denote rho (MatchingLogic.conj l) = M.denoteSet rho {delta | delta ∈ l}
@MatchingLogic.Model.denote_extendSignature_liftSignature : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type}

 (p : MatchingLogic.Pattern (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F) Var),
 (MatchingLogic.Model.extendSignature F M).denote rho (MatchingLogic.Pattern.liftSignature F p) = M.denote rho p
@MatchingLogic.Model.denote_extendSignature_restrictSignature : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type}
 [inst : DecidableEq Var] [inst_1 : DecidableEq S.Sym] (F : Finset S.Sym)
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model (MatchingLogic.Signature.restrict F)) (rho : Var → M.carrier)
 (p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) (h : p.symbolSupport ⊆ F),
 (MatchingLogic.Model.extendSignature F M).denote rho p = M.denote rho (MatchingLogic.Pattern.restrictSignature F p h)
@MatchingLogic.Model.denote_renameEquiv : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var Var' : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 [inst_1 : DecidableEq Var'] (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (e : Var ≃ Var') (rho : Var' → M.carrier)
 (p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var), M.denote rho (MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename (⇑e) p) = M.denote (rho ∘ ⇑e) p

 [inst_1 : DecidableEq Var'] (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (f : Var → Var'),
 Function.Injective f →
 ∀ (rho : Var' → M.carrier) (p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),
 M.denote rho (MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename f p) = M.denote (rho ∘ f) p
@MatchingLogic.Model.denote_substVar_of_captureFree : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} (M : MatchingLogic.Model S)

 M.denote rho (MatchingLogic.substVar x y p) = M.denote (Function.update rho x (rho y)) p
@MatchingLogic.Model.mk.sizeOf_spec : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} (carrier : Type) (nonempty : Nonempty carrier)
 (interp : (σ : S.Sym) → (Fin (S.arity σ) → carrier) → Set carrier),
 sizeOf { carrier := carrier, nonempty := nonempty, interp := interp } = 1 + sizeOf carrier + sizeOf nonempty

 (p q : List (MatchingLogic.Coord S)) (u v : M.carrier),
 M.reachWord (p ++ q) u v ↔ ∃ w, M.reachWord p u w ∧ M.reachWord q w v
@MatchingLogic.Model.reachWord_cons : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} (M : MatchingLogic.Model S)
 (e : MatchingLogic.Coord S) (p : List (MatchingLogic.Coord S)) (u v : M.carrier),
 M.reachWord (e :: p) u v ↔ ∃ w, M.stepAt e u w ∧ M.reachWord p w v

@MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem.app_mem : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {sigma : S.Sym}
 {Gamma : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} {phi : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ}

 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.app sigma fun i => MatchingLogic.conj (self.stages k i)) ∈ ↑Gamma
@MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem.base : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {sigma : S.Sym}

 {enum : ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ} (self : MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem sigma Gamma phi enum)
 (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), phi i ∈ self.stages 0 i
@MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem.canonicalExistence_of_stageSystem : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {sigma : S.Sym}
 {Gamma : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} {phi : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ}

 Function.Surjective enum →
 ∃ components,
 (∀ (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), phi i ∈ ↑(components i)) ∧ Gamma ∈ MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp sigma components

@MatchingLogic.NaryStageSystem.mk.sizeOf_spec : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {sigma : S.Sym}
 {Gamma : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} {phi : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ}
 {enum : ℕ → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ} (stages : ℕ → Fin (S.arity sigma) → List (MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ))
 (base : ∀ (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), phi i ∈ stages 0 i)
 (mono : ∀ (k : ℕ) (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), ∀ q ∈ stages k i, q ∈ stages (k + 1) i)
 (app_mem : ∀ (k : ℕ), (MatchingLogic.Pattern.app sigma fun i => MatchingLogic.conj (stages k i)) ∈ ↑Gamma)
 (decide : ∀ (k : ℕ) (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), enum k ∈ stages (k + 1) i ∨ (enum k).nt ∈ stages (k + 1) i)
 (witness :
 ∀ (k : ℕ) (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)) (x : ℕ) (p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ),
 enum k = MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p →
 ∃ y ∉ p.allVars,
 (MatchingLogic.Pattern.ex x p).imp (MatchingLogic.Pattern.captureAvoidingSubst x y p) ∈ stages (k + 1) i),
 sizeOf { stages := stages, base := base, mono := mono, app_mem := app_mem, decide := decide, witness := witness } = 1

 ((MatchingLogic.Pattern.app sigma Phi).imp

 (point : MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root),
 (∃ world, point = MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root world) ∨
 ∃ (hmissing : MatchingLogic.Missing root), point = MatchingLogic.completedStar hmissing
@MatchingLogic.completedEmbed_injective : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S),
 Function.Injective (MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root)
@MatchingLogic.completedEmbed_mem_completedInterp_iff : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature}
 (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) {sigma : S.Sym}
 (components : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root)
 (world : MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root),
 (MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root world ∈
 MatchingLogic.completedInterp root sigma fun i => MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root (components i)) ↔
 world ∈ MatchingLogic.generatedInterp root sigma components
@MatchingLogic.completedInterp_eq_empty_of_input_star : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature}
 {root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} {sigma : S.Sym}
 {inputs : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root} (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)),
 (inputs i).isStar → MatchingLogic.completedInterp root sigma inputs = ∅
@MatchingLogic.completedModel_interp : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S)
 (sigma : S.Sym) (inputs : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root),
 (MatchingLogic.completedModel root).interp sigma inputs = MatchingLogic.completedInterp root sigma inputs
@MatchingLogic.completedStar_mem_completedInterp_iff : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature}
 {root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} (hmissing : MatchingLogic.Missing root) {sigma : S.Sym}
 (components : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root),
 (MatchingLogic.completedStar hmissing ∈
 MatchingLogic.completedInterp root sigma fun i => MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root (components i)) ↔
 ∃ i, components i = MatchingLogic.generatedRoot root
@MatchingLogic.completedStar_ne_embed : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S}
 (hmissing : MatchingLogic.Missing root) (world : MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root),
 MatchingLogic.completedStar hmissing ≠ MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root world
@MatchingLogic.completedValuation_eq_embed_iff : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature}
 (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) (x : ℕ) (world : MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root),
 MatchingLogic.completedValuation root x = MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root world ↔
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.var x ∈ ↑↑world
@MatchingLogic.completedValuation_isStar_iff : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S)
 (x : ℕ),
 (MatchingLogic.completedValuation root x).isStar ↔
 ∀ (world : MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root), MatchingLogic.Pattern.var x ∉ ↑↑world
@MatchingLogic.completedValuation_surjective : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature}
 (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S), Function.Surjective (MatchingLogic.completedValuation root)
@MatchingLogic.completed_truth : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature},
 MatchingLogic.CanonicalExistenceProperty S →
 ∀ (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S) (world : MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root)
 (p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ),
 p ∈ ↑↑world ↔
 MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root world ∈
 (MatchingLogic.completedModel root).denote (MatchingLogic.completedValuation root) p
@MatchingLogic.countertheory_locConsistent_of_not_provable : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type}
 [inst : DecidableEq Var] {l : List (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)} {phi : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var},
 ¬MatchingLogic.Provable ∅ ((MatchingLogic.conj l).imp phi) →
 MatchingLogic.LocConsistent {delta | delta ∈ [MatchingLogic.conj l, phi.nt]}
@MatchingLogic.cover_denote_closed : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (C : Set M.carrier),
 M.BackwardClosed C →
 ∀ (hne : C.Nonempty),
 ∀ star ∉ C,
 ∀ {ψ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var},
 MatchingLogic.Closed ψ →
 ∀ (ν : Var → ↑C × Bool) (ρ : Var → M.carrier) (p : ↑C × Bool),
 p ∈ (MatchingLogic.cover M C hne).denote ν ψ ↔ ↑p.1 ∈ M.denote ρ ψ
@MatchingLogic.cover_sat_iff : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (C : Set M.carrier),
 M.BackwardClosed C →
 ∀ (hne : C.Nonempty),
 ∀ star ∉ C,
 ∀ {ψ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var},
 MatchingLogic.Closed ψ → ∀ (ρ : Var → M.carrier), (MatchingLogic.cover M C hne).Sat ψ ↔ C ⊆ M.denote ρ ψ
@MatchingLogic.definedness_conservative : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var],
 MatchingLogic.StrongLocalCompleteness S Var →
 ∀ {Γ : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)} {φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var},
 (∀ γ ∈ Γ, MatchingLogic.Closed γ) →
 MatchingLogic.Closed φ →
 ∀ (x : Var),
 MatchingLogic.Provable (MatchingLogic.emb '' Γ ∪ {MatchingLogic.definednessAxiom x}) (MatchingLogic.emb φ) →
 MatchingLogic.Provable Γ φ
@MatchingLogic.denoteSet_localize : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (ρ : Var → M.carrier) (Γ : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)),
 M.denoteSet ρ (MatchingLogic.localize Γ) =
 {u | ∀ (v : M.carrier), Relation.ReflTransGen M.Step u v → v ∈ M.denoteSet ρ Γ}

@MatchingLogic.denote_al : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (ρ : Var → M.carrier) (x : Var) (φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),
 M.denote ρ (MatchingLogic.Pattern.al x φ) = ⋂ a, M.denote (Function.update ρ x a) φ
@MatchingLogic.denote_app : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (ρ : Var → M.carrier) (σ : S.Sym) (f : Fin (S.arity σ) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),
 M.denote ρ (MatchingLogic.Pattern.app σ f) = M.app σ fun i => M.denote ρ (f i)

@MatchingLogic.denote_box : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]

 (ψ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),

 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) {φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var},

@MatchingLogic.denote_congr : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) (ρ ρ' : Var → M.carrier),

@MatchingLogic.denote_defined : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]

 ((MatchingLogic.expand M).denote ρ (MatchingLogic.defined φ) = Set.univ ↔
 ((MatchingLogic.expand M).denote ρ φ).Nonempty) ∧
 ((MatchingLogic.expand M).denote ρ (MatchingLogic.defined φ) = ∅ ↔ (MatchingLogic.expand M).denote ρ φ = ∅)

@MatchingLogic.localCons_compact : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 {Delta : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)} {phi : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var},

 ∃ l, (∀ delta ∈ l, delta ∈ Delta) ∧ MatchingLogic.LocalCons {delta | delta ∈ l} phi
@MatchingLogic.localCons_list_iff : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 (l : List (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)) (phi : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),
 MatchingLogic.LocalCons {delta | delta ∈ l} phi ↔
 ∀ (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (rho : Var → M.carrier), M.denote rho (MatchingLogic.conj l) ⊆ M.denote rho phi
@MatchingLogic.localCons_renameEquiv : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var Var' : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 [inst_1 : DecidableEq Var'] (e : Var ≃ Var') {Delta : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)}
 {phi : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var},
 MatchingLogic.LocalCons Delta phi ↔
 MatchingLogic.LocalCons (MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename ⇑e '' Delta) (MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename (⇑e) phi)
@MatchingLogic.localCons_renameInjective : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var Var' : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]

 MatchingLogic.LocalCons Delta phi ↔
 MatchingLogic.LocalCons (MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename f '' Delta) (MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename f phi)
@MatchingLogic.locality : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) {C : Set M.carrier},
 M.BackwardClosed C →
 ∀ (ψ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) (ρ ρ' : Var → M.carrier),
 MatchingLogic.AgreeOn C ρ ρ' → M.denote ρ ψ ∩ C = M.denote ρ' ψ ∩ C
MatchingLogic.localize_not_redundant : ¬∀ (S : MatchingLogic.Signature) (Var : Type) (x : DecidableEq Var)
 (Γ : Set (MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var)) (φ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),
 (∀ γ ∈ Γ, MatchingLogic.Closed γ) →
 MatchingLogic.Closed φ → (MatchingLogic.GlobalCons Γ φ ↔ MatchingLogic.LocalCons Γ φ)
@MatchingLogic.mem_canonicalInterp : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {sigma : S.Sym}
 {components : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S} {Gamma : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S},
 Gamma ∈ MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp sigma components ↔
 ∀ (args : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ),
 (∀ (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), args i ∈ ↑(components i)) → MatchingLogic.Pattern.app sigma args ∈ ↑Gamma
@MatchingLogic.mem_canonicalModel_interp : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} (root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S)
 {sigma : S.Sym} {components : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S}
 {Gamma : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S},
 Gamma ∈ (MatchingLogic.canonicalModel root).interp sigma components ↔
 ∀ (args : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.Pattern S ℕ),
 (∀ (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), args i ∈ ↑(components i)) → MatchingLogic.Pattern.app sigma args ∈ ↑Gamma
@MatchingLogic.mem_completedInterp : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S}
 {sigma : S.Sym} {inputs : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root}
 {output : MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root},
 output ∈ MatchingLogic.completedInterp root sigma inputs ↔
 ∃ components,
 (∀ (i : Fin (S.arity sigma)), inputs i = MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root (components i)) ∧
 ((∃ world,
 output = MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root world ∧
 world ∈ MatchingLogic.generatedInterp root sigma components) ∨
 output.isStar ∧ ∃ i, components i = MatchingLogic.generatedRoot root)
@MatchingLogic.mem_completedModel_interp : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S}
 {sigma : S.Sym} {inputs : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root}
 {output : MatchingLogic.CompletedCarrier root},
 output ∈ (MatchingLogic.completedModel root).interp sigma inputs ↔
 output ∈ MatchingLogic.completedInterp root sigma inputs
@MatchingLogic.mem_generatedInterp : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S}
 {sigma : S.Sym} {components : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root}
 {world : MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root},
 world ∈ MatchingLogic.generatedInterp root sigma components ↔
 ↑world ∈ MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp sigma fun i => ↑(components i)
@MatchingLogic.mem_generatedModel_interp : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {root : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier S}
 {sigma : S.Sym} {components : Fin (S.arity sigma) → MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root}
 {world : MatchingLogic.GeneratedCarrier root},
 world ∈ (MatchingLogic.generatedModel root).interp sigma components ↔
 ↑world ∈ MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp sigma fun i => ↑(components i)

 MatchingLogic.Provable Γ ψ → ∀ (p : List (MatchingLogic.Coord S)), MatchingLogic.Provable Γ (MatchingLogic.boxes p ψ)

@MatchingLogic.two_copies : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (C : Set M.carrier),
 M.BackwardClosed C →
 ∀ (hne : C.Nonempty),
 ∀ star ∉ C,
 ∀ (ψ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) (ν : Var → ↑C × Bool) (p : ↑C × Bool),
 p ∈ (MatchingLogic.cover M C hne).denote ν ψ ↔
 ↑p.1 ∈ M.denote (fun x => MatchingLogic.proj M C star p.2 (ν x)) ψ
@MatchingLogic.two_copies_set : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var : Type} [inst : DecidableEq Var]
 (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (C : Set M.carrier),
 M.BackwardClosed C →
 ∀ (hne : C.Nonempty),
 ∀ star ∉ C,
 ∀ (ψ : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var) (ν : Var → ↑C × Bool),
 (MatchingLogic.cover M C hne).denote ν ψ =
 {p | p.2 = false ∧ ↑p.1 ∈ M.denote (fun x => MatchingLogic.proj M C star false (ν x)) ψ} ∪
 {p | p.2 = true ∧ ↑p.1 ∈ M.denote (fun x => MatchingLogic.proj M C star true (ν x)) ψ}

@MatchingLogic.Model.app_const : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} (M : MatchingLogic.Model S) (σ : S.Sym)

 (e : MatchingLogic.Coord S) (p : List (MatchingLogic.Coord S)) (u v : M.carrier),

@MatchingLogic.Pattern.substVar_rename_injective : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var Var' : Type}

 Function.Injective f →
 ∀ (x y : Var) (p : MatchingLogic.Pattern S Var),
 MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename f (MatchingLogic.substVar x y p) =
 MatchingLogic.substVar (f x) (f y) (MatchingLogic.Pattern.rename f p)
@MatchingLogic.Pattern.captureFree_rename_injective : ∀ {S : MatchingLogic.Signature} {Var Var' : Type}

 (Gamma : MatchingLogic.CanonicalCarrier MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSig),
 MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.nullaryPattern ∈ ↑Gamma →

 (∀ (i : Fin (MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSig.arity MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.nullary)),

 Gamma ∈ MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.nullary components

 MatchingLogic.Pattern.var 0 ∈ ↑(components i)) ∧
 Gamma ∈ MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.unary components

 root ∈ MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.nullary components

 root ∈ MatchingLogic.canonicalInterp MatchingLogic.EntryIIIRegression.AuditSym.unary components

 MatchingLogic.completedEmbed root (MatchingLogic.generatedRoot root) ∈ Set.univ
